(herald
  "Verification of TLS 1.3 security properties proven in Tamarin analysis")

(comment "CPSA 4.4.8")
(comment "All input read from tls13_m2.scm")

(defprotocol tls13 basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defskeleton tls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk akey) (s ca name)
    (x rndx) (y expt))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y))
  (non-orig (invk spk) (privk ca))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x)
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y) (non (invk spk))
          (non (privk ca)) (ugen x) (uniq-at cr z 0))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "server" z-0 4) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)
            (p "server" "s" z-0 s) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
            (p "server" "y" z-0 y) (p "server" "x" z-0 x))))))
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))))
  (label 0)
  (unrealized (0 1))
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y)
        (request request) (response response))))
  (origs (cr (0 0)))
  (ugens (x (0 0)))
  (comment "1 in cohort - 1 not yet seen"))

(defskeleton tls13
  (vars (cr sr cr-0 sr-0 random32) (request response text) (spk akey)
    (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt) (y-0 rndx) (x-0 expt))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y))
  (defstrand server 2 (cr cr-0) (sr sr-0) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca)
    (y y-0) (x x-0))
  (precedes ((1 1) (0 1)))
  (non-orig (invk spk) (privk ca))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x y-0)
  (absent (y-0 x-0))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y) (non (invk spk))
          (non (privk ca)) (ugen x) (uniq-at cr z 0))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "server" z-0 4) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)
            (p "server" "s" z-0 s) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
            (p "server" "y" z-0 y) (p "server" "x" z-0 x))))))
  (operation encryption-test (added-strand server 2)
    (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)) (0 1))
  (strand-map 0)
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    ((recv (cat cr-0 (exp (gen) x-0)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y-0 x-0)) "s hs traffic" cr-0
                  sr-0)) (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y-0 x-0)) "s hs traffic" cr-0
              sr-0))))))
  (label 1)
  (parent 0)
  (unrealized (0 1))
  (maps
    ((0)
      ((cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y)
        (request request) (response response))))
  (comment "2 in cohort - 2 not yet seen"))

(defskeleton tls13
  (vars (cr cr-0 sr random32) (request response text) (spk akey)
    (s ca name) (x rndx) (x-0 expt) (y rndx))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y))
  (defstrand server 2 (cr cr-0) (sr sr) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (y y)
    (x x-0))
  (precedes ((1 1) (0 1)))
  (non-orig (invk spk) (privk ca))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x y)
  (absent (y x-0))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)

            (p "server" "y" z-0 y) (p "server" "x" z-0 x))))))
  (operation encryption-test (displaced 2 1 server 2)
    (enc
      (hash (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y-0)) s spk
        (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)) (0 1))
  (strand-map 0 1)

              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))

              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))

              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))

        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))

          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr

        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))

(defskeleton tls13
  (vars (cr sr cr-0 sr-0 cr-1 random32) (request response text)
    (spk akey) (s ca name) (x y rndx) (x-0 x-1 expt) (y-0 rndx))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y-0))
  (defstrand server 2 (cr cr-0) (sr sr-0) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (y y)
    (x x-0))
  (defstrand server 2 (cr cr-1) (sr sr) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (y y-0)
    (x x-1))
  (precedes ((1 1) (0 1)) ((2 1) (0 1)))
  (non-orig (invk spk) (privk ca))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen x y y-0)
  (absent (y x-0) (y-0 x-1))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y) (non (invk spk))
          (non (privk ca)) (ugen x) (uniq-at cr z 0))
        (exists ((z-0 strd))
          (and (p "server" z-0 4) (p "server" "cr" z-0 cr)
            (p "server" "sr" z-0 sr) (p "server" "spk" z-0 spk)
            (p "server" "s" z-0 s) (p "server" "ca" z-0 ca)
            (p "server" "y" z-0 y) (p "server" "x" z-0 x))))))
  (operation encryption-test (added-strand server 2)
    (enc
      (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0)) s spk
        (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)) (0 1))
  (strand-map 0 1)
  (traces
    ((send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y-0)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y-0)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y-0)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y-0)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y-0)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y-0)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y-0)) "derived")))))
    ((recv (cat cr-0 (exp (gen) x-0)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x-0)) "s hs traffic" cr-0 sr-0))
              (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr-0 (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x-0)) "s hs traffic" cr-0 sr-0)))))
    ((recv (cat cr-1 (exp (gen) x-1)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x-1 y-0)) "s hs traffic" cr-1 sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y-0)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))

(defskeleton tls13
  (vars (cr sr random32) (request response text) (spk akey) (s ca name)
    (y x rndx))
  (defstrand client 5 (cr cr) (sr sr) (request request)
    (response response) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (x x) (y y))
  (defstrand server 2 (cr cr) (sr sr) (spk spk) (s s) (ca ca) (y y)
    (x x))
  (precedes ((0 0) (1 0)) ((1 1) (0 1)))
  (non-orig (invk spk) (privk ca))
  (uniq-orig cr)
  (uniq-gen y x)
  (absent (y x))
  (goals
    (forall
      ((cr sr random32) (spk akey) (s ca name) (x rndx) (y expt)
        (z strd))
      (implies
        (and (p "client" z 5) (p "client" "cr" z cr)
          (p "client" "sr" z sr) (p "client" "spk" z spk)
          (p "client" "s" z s) (p "client" "ca" z ca)
          (p "client" "x" z x) (p "client" "y" z y) (non (invk spk))
          (non (privk ca)) (ugen x) (uniq-at cr z 0))

  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt) (spk akey)

  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))

              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))

                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))

              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))

                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk

            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (hash

              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))

              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk

            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))

    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))

(defprotocol tls13 basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk)))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s spk (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s spk
                  (enc (hash s spk) (privk ca))) (invk spk))))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x)))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (lang (random32 atom)))

(defprotocol tls13fs basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                  (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                  (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv s spk (invk spk)))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                  (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                  (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x))
    (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))
  (defrole cert-gen
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn) (ignore mesg))
    (trace (load priv-stor ignore)
      (stor priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk))))
    (uniq-gen pk))
  (defrole privkey-disclose
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk)))
      (stor priv-stor "nil") (send pk))
    (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))
  (defrule undisclosed-not-disclosed
    (forall ((z strd) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk) (fact undisclosed pk))
        (false))))
  (defrule cert-gen-once-interference
    (forall ((z1 z2 strd) (self name))
      (implies
        (and (p "cert-gen" z2 (idx 2)) (p "cert-gen" z1 (idx 2))
          (p "cert-gen" "self" z2 self) (p "cert-gen" "self" z1 self)
          (fact cert-gen-once self))
        (= z1 z2))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule eff-server-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "server" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-server-0
    (forall ((z strd) (s name) (spk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s)) (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0
    (forall ((z strd) (self name) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 1))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z self))
        (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))))
  (lang (random32 atom) (pv (tuple 3))))

                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)

              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)

(defprotocol tls13fs basic
  (defrole client
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (x rndx) (y expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text))
    (trace (send (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (recv
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                  (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (send
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                  (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (send
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived"))))
      (recv
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul x y)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen x))
  (defrole server
    (vars (s ca name) (cr sr random32) (y rndx) (x expt) (spk akey)
      (request response text) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv s spk (invk spk)))
      (recv (cat cr (exp (gen) x)))
      (send
        (cat (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
          (enc (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                  (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk))
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))))
      (recv
        (enc
          (hash
            (hash "finished"
              (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr))
            (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
            (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
            (enc
              (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)
            (hash
              (hash "finished"
                (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "s hs traffic" cr sr))
              (cat sr (exp (gen) y))
              (cat s (invk spk) (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca)))
              (enc
                (hash (cat sr (exp (gen) y)) s (invk spk)
                  (enc (hash s (invk spk)) (privk ca))) spk)))
          (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "c hs traffic" cr sr)))
      (recv
        (enc request
          (hash "c ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived"))))
      (send
        (enc response
          (hash "s ap traffic" cr sr
            (hash (exp (gen) (mul y x)) "derived")))))
    (uniq-gen y)
    (absent (y x))
    (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))
  (defrole cert-gen
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn) (ignore mesg))
    (trace (load priv-stor ignore)
      (stor priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk))))
    (uniq-gen pk))
  (defrole privkey-disclose
    (vars (self name) (pk akey) (priv-stor locn))
    (trace (load priv-stor (pv self pk (invk pk)))
      (stor priv-stor "nil") (send pk))
    (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))
  (defrule undisclosed-not-disclosed
    (forall ((z strd) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk) (fact undisclosed pk))
        (false))))
  (defrule cert-gen-once-interference
    (forall ((z1 z2 strd) (self name))
      (implies
        (and (p "cert-gen" z2 (idx 2)) (p "cert-gen" z1 (idx 2))
          (p "cert-gen" "self" z2 self) (p "cert-gen" "self" z1 self)
          (fact cert-gen-once self))
        (= z1 z2))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_indx
    (forall ((x indx)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_strd
    (forall ((x strd)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule neqRl_mesg
    (forall ((x mesg)) (implies (fact neq x x) (false))))
  (defgenrule scissorsRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (trans z2 i2)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)))))
  (defgenrule cakeRule
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)) (false))))
  (defgenrule no-interruption
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (leads-to z0 i0 z2 i2) (trans z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2))
        (false))))
  (defgenrule discreteAfter
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z2 i2) (leads-to z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (same-locn z0 i0 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z1 z2) (= i1 i2)) (prec z1 i1 z2 i2)))))
  (defgenrule discreteBefore
    (forall ((z0 z1 z2 strd) (i0 i1 i2 indx))
      (implies
        (and (trans z0 i0) (trans z1 i1) (same-locn z0 i0 z1 i1)
          (leads-to z1 i1 z2 i2) (prec z0 i0 z2 i2))
        (or (and (= z0 z1) (= i0 i1)) (prec z0 i0 z1 i1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_cert-gen-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "cert-gen" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-1
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 1)))))
  (defgenrule trRl_privkey-disclose-at-0
    (forall ((z strd))
      (implies (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 2)) (trans z (idx 0)))))
  (defgenrule eff-server-1
    (forall ((i indx) (z1 z strd))
      (implies (and (p "server" z (idx 2)) (prec z (idx 1) z1 i))
        (or (= z z1)
          (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (prec z (idx 0) z1 i))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-server-0
    (forall ((z strd) (s name) (spk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "server" z (idx 1)) (p "server" "spk" z spk)
          (p "server" "s" z s)) (gen-st (pv s spk (invk spk))))))
  (defgenrule gen-st-privkey-disclose-0
    (forall ((z strd) (self name) (pk akey))
      (implies
        (and (p "privkey-disclose" z (idx 1))
          (p "privkey-disclose" "pk" z pk)
          (p "privkey-disclose" "self" z self))
        (gen-st (pv self pk (invk pk))))))
  (lang (random32 atom) (pv (tuple 3))))
